# GitHub Issue Sentiment Analysis with Composio Triggers and Workbench ## Background Composio Triggers allow your application to react to external events in real-time. The Composio Workbench provides a sandboxed environment to run complex data processing scripts. In this task, you will build an automated pipeline that listens for new GitHub issues and uses the Workbench to analyze their sentiment. ## Requirements * Create a Python script named `sentiment_pipeline.py` in `/home/user/sentiment-task`. * The script must use the `composio` Python SDK and the user `zealt-user01`. * **Step 1: Create a Trigger** * Create a trigger for the event `GITHUB_ISSUE_OPENED_EVENT` (or similar, search for the correct slug). * Configure it for the repository `composiohq/composio`. * **Step 2: Handle Events** * Use `composio.triggers.subscribe()` to listen for events. * When a new issue is opened, extract the issue body. * **Step 3: Analyze Sentiment via Workbench** * Use the `COMPOSIO_REMOTE_WORKBENCH` meta-tool (via a session) to execute a Python script in the sandbox. * The sandbox script should use a simple heuristic or a library (like `textblob` if available, or just keyword matching) to determine if the sentiment is 'negative'. * If negative, the sandbox script should call `run_composio_tool('GITHUB_ADD_LABELS_TO_AN_ISSUE', ...)` to add the `urgent` label to the issue. * The script should print "Pipeline active" and wait for events. ## Implementation Guide 1. Initialize `Composio`. 2. Create a session for `zealt-user01` to access meta-tools. 3. Create the GitHub trigger. 4. Define an event handler that gets the issue details. 5. In the handler, call `session.execute('COMPOSIO_REMOTE_WORKBENCH', ...)` with the analysis code. 6. The workbench code has access to `run_composio_tool`. ## Constraints * Project path: /home/user/sentiment-task * Language: Python * User ID: `zealt-user01` * Repository: `composiohq/composio` ## Integrations * Composio * GitHub

## 📦 What Was Built

**Main Script:** `sentiment_pipeline.py` - A complete Python script that:
- Uses Composio SDK with user `zealt-user01`
- Creates a trigger for `GITHUB_ISSUE_OPENED_EVENT` on `composiohq/composio` repository
- Listens for new GitHub issues in real-time
- Extracts issue body and analyzes sentiment using Composio Workbench
- Automatically adds 'urgent' label to issues with negative sentiment
- Uses keyword matching for sentiment detection (bug, error, fail, crash, urgent, critical, etc.)

**Documentation:**
- `README.md` - Comprehensive documentation with setup, usage, and troubleshooting
- `QUICKSTART.md` - Quick start guide for rapid deployment

**Configuration:**
- `config.example.py` - Example configuration with customizable settings
- `requirements.txt` - Python dependencies
- `.gitignore` - Protects sensitive information

## 🚀 Quick Start

```bash
cd /home/user/sentiment-task
pip install -r requirements.txt
export COMPOSIO_API_KEY="your-api-key"
python sentiment_pipeline.py
```

The script will display "Pipeline active" and automatically monitor for new issues, analyzing their sentiment and labeling urgent ones as they're created on the `composiohq/composio` repository.
